Complex Join Benchmark: Category X

MongoDB $lookup/$graphLookup vs Oracle JSON JOIN / Relational JOIN / CTE / Property Graph

MongoDB Wins

0

Oracle A Wins

3

Oracle B Wins

27
X0: Many-to-Many
Products ↔ Categories via junction table. MongoDB uses two $lookup stages with $unwind. Oracle uses 3-table JOIN.
$lookup
Oracle JSON
Oracle Relational
TestNotes$lookup (ms)Oracle JSON (ms)Oracle Relational (ms)Winner
X0_M2M_basic_1K1K products, 50 categories, ~3 cats/product40.867.791.91Oracle Relational
X0_M2M_basic_10K10K products, 100 categories, ~3 cats/product385.9069.2912.90Oracle Relational
X0_M2M_basic_100K100K products, 500 categories, ~3 cats/product3973.95708.50127.42Oracle Relational
X0_M2M_dense10K products, 100 categories, 20 cats/product1890.40455.3276.35Oracle Relational
X0_M2M_sparse10K products, 100 categories, 2 cats/product304.3365.3212.91Oracle Relational
X0_M2M_basic_1K — M:N Basic 1K
Pipeline JSON
[
  {
  "$lookup": {
    "from": "benchmark_product_categories",
    "localField": "_id",
    "foreignField": "product_id",
    "as": "pc"
  }
},
  {
  "$unwind": "$pc"
},
  {
  "$lookup": {
    "from": "benchmark_categories",
    "localField": "pc.category_id",
    "foreignField": "_id",
    "as": "category"
  }
},
  {
  "$unwind": "$category"
}
]

X1_hier_2level — Oracle JSON
SELECT c.data, o.data FROM benchmark_customers c JOIN benchmark_orders o ON JSON_VALUE(c.data, '$._id') = JSON_VALUE(o.data, '$.customer_id')
X1_hier_3level — Oracle JSON
SELECT c.data, o.data, i.data FROM benchmark_customers c JOIN benchmark_orders o ON JSON_VALUE(c.data, '$._id') = JSON_VALUE(o.data, '$.customer_id') JOIN benchmark_order_items i ON JSON_VALUE(o.data, '$._id') = JSON_VALUE(i.data, '$.order_id')
X1_hier_4level — Oracle JSON
SELECT c.data, o.data, i.data, s.data FROM benchmark_customers c JOIN benchmark_orders o ON JSON_VALUE(c.data, '$._id') = JSON_VALUE(o.data, '$.customer_id') JOIN benchmark_order_items i ON JSON_VALUE(o.data, '$._id') = JSON_VALUE(i.data, '$.order_id') LEFT JOIN benchmark_shipments s ON JSON_VALUE(i.data, '$._id') = JSON_VALUE(s.data, '$.item_id')
X1_hier_wide — Oracle JSON
SELECT c.data, o.data FROM benchmark_customers c JOIN benchmark_orders o ON JSON_VALUE(c.data, '$._id') = JSON_VALUE(o.data, '$.customer_id')
X1_hier_deep — Oracle JSON
SELECT c.data, o.data, i.data, s.data FROM benchmark_customers c JOIN benchmark_orders o ON JSON_VALUE(c.data, '$._id') = JSON_VALUE(o.data, '$.customer_id') JOIN benchmark_order_items i ON JSON_VALUE(o.data, '$._id') = JSON_VALUE(i.data, '$.order_id') LEFT JOIN benchmark_shipments s ON JSON_VALUE(i.data, '$._id') = JSON_VALUE(s.data, '$.item_id')
X1_hier_2level — Oracle Relational
SELECT c.customer_id, c.name, o.order_id, o.total FROM benchmark_customers_rel c JOIN benchmark_orders_rel o ON c.customer_id = o.customer_id
X1_hier_3level — Oracle Relational
SELECT c.customer_id, c.name, o.order_id, o.total, i.item_id, i.quantity FROM benchmark_customers_rel c JOIN benchmark_orders_rel o ON c.customer_id = o.customer_id JOIN benchmark_order_items_rel i ON o.order_id = i.order_id
X1_hier_4level — Oracle Relational
SELECT c.customer_id, c.name, o.order_id, o.total, i.item_id, i.quantity, s.shipment_id, s.status FROM benchmark_customers_rel c JOIN benchmark_orders_rel o ON c.customer_id = o.customer_id JOIN benchmark_order_items_rel i ON o.order_id = i.order_id LEFT JOIN benchmark_shipments_rel s ON i.item_id = s.item_id
X1_hier_wide — Oracle Relational
SELECT c.customer_id, c.name, o.order_id, o.total FROM benchmark_customers_rel c JOIN benchmark_orders_rel o ON c.customer_id = o.customer_id
X1_hier_deep — Oracle Relational
SELECT c.customer_id, c.name, o.order_id, o.total, i.item_id, i.quantity, s.shipment_id, s.status FROM benchmark_customers_rel c JOIN benchmark_orders_rel o ON c.customer_id = o.customer_id JOIN benchmark_order_items_rel i ON o.order_id = i.order_id LEFT JOIN benchmark_shipments_rel s ON i.item_id = s.item_id

X2_M2N_child1N_aggregate — M:N+Child Aggregate
Pipeline JSON
[
  {
  "$lookup": {
    "from": "benchmark_order_items",
    "localField": "_id",
    "foreignField": "product_id",
    "as": "items"
  }
},
  {
  "$unwind": "$items"
},
  {
  "$lookup": {
    "from": "benchmark_shipments",
    "localField": "items._id",
    "foreignField": "item_id",
    "as": "shipments"
  }
},
  {
  "$group": {
    "_id": "$_id",
    "name": {
      "$first": "$name"
    },
    "item_count": {
      "$sum": 1
    },
    "total_qty": {
      "$sum": "$items.quantity"
    },
    "ship_count": {
      "$sum": {
        "$size": "$shipments"
      }
    }
  }
}
]

SELECT p.data, i.data, s.data FROM benchmark_products p JOIN benchmark_order_items i ON JSON_VALUE(p.data, '$._id') = JSON_VALUE(i.data, '$.product_id') LEFT JOIN benchmark_shipments s ON JSON_VALUE(i.data, '$._id') = JSON_VALUE(s.data, '$.item_id')

X2_M2N_child1N_aggregate — Oracle JSON
SELECT JSON_VALUE(p.data, '$._id') AS product_id, JSON_VALUE(p.data, '$.name') AS product_name, COUNT(DISTINCT JSON_VALUE(i.data, '$._id')) AS item_count, SUM(JSON_VALUE(i.data, '$.quantity' RETURNING NUMBER)) AS total_qty, COUNT(DISTINCT JSON_VALUE(s.data, '$._id')) AS ship_count FROM benchmark_products p JOIN benchmark_order_items i ON JSON_VALUE(p.data, '$._id') = JSON_VALUE(i.data, '$.product_id') LEFT JOIN benchmark_shipments s ON JSON_VALUE(i.data, '$._id') = JSON_VALUE(s.data, '$.item_id') GROUP BY JSON_VALUE(p.data, '$._id'), JSON_VALUE(p.data, '$.name')
X2_M2N_child1N_basic — Oracle Relational

X2_M2N_child1N_aggregate — Oracle Relational
SELECT p.product_id, p.name, COUNT(DISTINCT i.item_id) AS item_count, SUM(i.quantity) AS total_qty, COUNT(DISTINCT s.shipment_id) AS ship_count FROM benchmark_products_rel p JOIN benchmark_order_items_rel i ON p.product_id = i.product_id LEFT JOIN benchmark_shipments_rel s ON i.item_id = s.item_id GROUP BY p.product_id, p.name

SELECT p.data, r.data, c.data, cr.data FROM benchmark_products p LEFT JOIN benchmark_reviews r ON JSON_VALUE(p.data, '$._id') = JSON_VALUE(r.data, '$.product_id') JOIN benchmark_product_categories pc ON JSON_VALUE(p.data, '$._id') = JSON_VALUE(pc.data, '$.product_id') JOIN benchmark_categories c ON JSON_VALUE(pc.data, '$.category_id') = JSON_VALUE(c.data, '$._id') LEFT JOIN benchmark_category_rules cr ON JSON_VALUE(c.data, '$._id') = JSON_VALUE(cr.data, '$.category_id')

X3_M2N_both1N_aggregate — Oracle JSON
SELECT JSON_VALUE(c.data, '$._id') AS category_id, JSON_VALUE(c.data, '$.name') AS cat_name, COUNT(DISTINCT JSON_VALUE(p.data, '$._id')) AS product_count, AVG(JSON_VALUE(r.data, '$.rating' RETURNING NUMBER)) AS avg_rating, COUNT(DISTINCT JSON_VALUE(r.data, '$._id')) AS review_count FROM benchmark_categories c JOIN benchmark_product_categories pc ON JSON_VALUE(c.data, '$._id') = JSON_VALUE(pc.data, '$.category_id') JOIN benchmark_products p ON JSON_VALUE(pc.data, '$.product_id') = JSON_VALUE(p.data, '$._id') LEFT JOIN benchmark_reviews r ON JSON_VALUE(p.data, '$._id') = JSON_VALUE(r.data, '$.product_id') GROUP BY JSON_VALUE(c.data, '$._id'), JSON_VALUE(c.data, '$.name')

X3_M2N_both1N_aggregate — Oracle Relational
SELECT c.category_id, c.name AS cat_name, COUNT(DISTINCT p.product_id) AS product_count, AVG(r.rating) AS avg_rating, COUNT(DISTINCT r.review_id) AS review_count FROM benchmark_categories_rel c JOIN benchmark_product_categories_rel pc ON c.category_id = pc.category_id JOIN benchmark_products_rel p ON pc.product_id = p.product_id LEFT JOIN benchmark_reviews_rel r ON p.product_id = r.product_id GROUP BY c.category_id, c.name

X4_selfref_2level — Recursive CTE
WITH category_tree (category_id, name, parent_id, lvl, depth) AS (  SELECT category_id, name, parent_id, lvl, 0 AS depth  FROM benchmark_categories_rel  WHERE parent_id IS NULL  UNION ALL  SELECT c.category_id, c.name, c.parent_id, c.lvl, ct.depth + 1  FROM benchmark_categories_rel c  JOIN category_tree ct ON c.parent_id = ct.category_id  WHERE ct.depth < 2) SELECT * FROM category_tree
X4_selfref_3level — Recursive CTE
WITH category_tree (category_id, name, parent_id, lvl, depth) AS (  SELECT category_id, name, parent_id, lvl, 0 AS depth  FROM benchmark_categories_rel  WHERE parent_id IS NULL  UNION ALL  SELECT c.category_id, c.name, c.parent_id, c.lvl, ct.depth + 1  FROM benchmark_categories_rel c  JOIN category_tree ct ON c.parent_id = ct.category_id  WHERE ct.depth < 3) SELECT * FROM category_tree
X4_selfref_5level — Recursive CTE

X4_selfref_wide — Recursive CTE
WITH category_tree (category_id, name, parent_id, lvl, depth) AS (  SELECT category_id, name, parent_id, lvl, 0 AS depth  FROM benchmark_categories_rel  WHERE parent_id IS NULL  UNION ALL  SELECT c.category_id, c.name, c.parent_id, c.lvl, ct.depth + 1  FROM benchmark_categories_rel c  JOIN category_tree ct ON c.parent_id = ct.category_id  WHERE ct.depth < 2) SELECT * FROM category_tree
X4_selfref_path — Recursive CTE
WITH path_cte (leaf_id, current_id, current_name, current_parent_id, depth) AS (  SELECT category_id, category_id, name, parent_id, 0  FROM benchmark_categories_rel  UNION ALL  SELECT p.leaf_id, c.category_id, c.name, c.parent_id, p.depth + 1  FROM path_cte p  JOIN benchmark_categories_rel c ON c.category_id = p.current_parent_id  WHERE p.current_parent_id IS NOT NULL AND p.depth < 10) SELECT leaf_id,   LISTAGG(current_name, ' > ') WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY depth DESC) AS full_path FROM path_cte GROUP BY leaf_id

X5_diamond_aggregate — Diamond Aggregate
Pipeline JSON
[
  {
  "$lookup": {
    "from": "benchmark_customers",
    "localField": "customer_id",
    "foreignField": "_id",
    "as": "customer"
  }
},
  {
  "$unwind": "$customer"
},
  {
  "$lookup": {
    "from": "benchmark_regions",
    "localField": "customer.region_id",
    "foreignField": "_id",
    "as": "cust_region"
  }
},
  {
  "$lookup": {
    "from": "benchmark_order_items",
    "localField": "_id",
    "foreignField": "order_id",
    "as": "items"
  }
},
  {
  "$unwind": "$items"
},
  {
  "$lookup": {
    "from": "benchmark_products",
    "localField": "items.product_id",
    "foreignField": "_id",
    "as": "product"
  }
},
  {
  "$unwind": "$product"
},
  {
  "$lookup": {
    "from": "benchmark_suppliers",
    "localField": "product.supplier_id",
    "foreignField": "_id",
    "as": "supplier"
  }
},
  {
  "$unwind": "$supplier"
},
  {
  "$lookup": {
    "from": "benchmark_regions",
    "localField": "supplier.region_id",
    "foreignField": "_id",
    "as": "supp_region"
  }
},
  {
  "$unwind": {
    "path": "$cust_region",
    "preserveNullAndEmptyArrays": true
  }
},
  {
  "$group": {
    "_id": {
      "cust_region": "$cust_region.name"
    },
    "order_count": {
      "$addToSet": "$_id"
    },
    "total_items": {
      "$sum": 1
    },
    "total_value": {
      "$sum": "$items.unit_price"
    }
  }
},
  {
  "$addFields": {
    "order_count": {
      "$size": "$order_count"
    }
  }
}
]

X5_diamond_basic — Oracle JSON
SELECT o.data, c.data, cr.data, i.data, p.data, s.data, sr.data FROM benchmark_orders o JOIN benchmark_customers c ON JSON_VALUE(o.data, '$.customer_id') = JSON_VALUE(c.data, '$._id') JOIN benchmark_regions cr ON JSON_VALUE(c.data, '$.region_id') = JSON_VALUE(cr.data, '$._id') JOIN benchmark_order_items i ON JSON_VALUE(o.data, '$._id') = JSON_VALUE(i.data, '$.order_id') JOIN benchmark_products p ON JSON_VALUE(i.data, '$.product_id') = JSON_VALUE(p.data, '$._id') JOIN benchmark_suppliers s ON JSON_VALUE(p.data, '$.supplier_id') = JSON_VALUE(s.data, '$._id') JOIN benchmark_regions sr ON JSON_VALUE(s.data, '$.region_id') = JSON_VALUE(sr.data, '$._id')

X5_diamond_aggregate — Oracle JSON
SELECT JSON_VALUE(cr.data, '$.name') AS region_name, COUNT(DISTINCT JSON_VALUE(o.data, '$._id')) AS order_count, COUNT(JSON_VALUE(i.data, '$._id')) AS total_items, SUM(JSON_VALUE(i.data, '$.unit_price' RETURNING NUMBER)) AS total_value FROM benchmark_orders o JOIN benchmark_customers c ON JSON_VALUE(o.data, '$.customer_id') = JSON_VALUE(c.data, '$._id') JOIN benchmark_regions cr ON JSON_VALUE(c.data, '$.region_id') = JSON_VALUE(cr.data, '$._id') JOIN benchmark_order_items i ON JSON_VALUE(o.data, '$._id') = JSON_VALUE(i.data, '$.order_id') GROUP BY JSON_VALUE(cr.data, '$.name')
X5_diamond_filtered — Oracle JSON
